[h2]binarySearch public static int binarySearch​(double[] a, int fromIndex, int toIndex, double key) Searches a range of the specified array of doubles for the specified value using the binary search algorithm. The range must be sorted (as by the [sort(double[],int,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrays-0000001054678559#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678559__sort-double:A-int-int-) method) prior to making this call. If it is not sorted, the results are undefined. If the range contains multiple elements with the specified value, there is no guarantee which one will be found. This method considers all NaN values to be equivalent and equal. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  the array to be searched  
Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
a  |  the array to be sorted  
c  |  the comparator to determine the order of the array. A null value indicates that the elements' [Comparable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/comparable-0000001054558443) should be used.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[ClassCastException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/classcastexception-0000001054678419) |  if the array contains elements that are not _mutually comparable_ using the specified comparator  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  (optional) if the comparator is found to violate the [Comparator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/comparator-0000001054558585) contract  
[h2]sort public static <T> void sort​(T[] a, int fromIndex, int toIndex, [Comparator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/comparator-0000001054558585)<? super T> c) Sorts the specified range of the specified array of objects according to the order induced by the specified comparator. The range to be sorted extends from index fromIndex, inclusive, to index toIndex, exclusive. (If fromIndex==toIndex, the range to be sorted is empty.) All elements in the range must be _mutually comparable_ by the specified comparator (that is, c.compare(e1, e2) must not throw a ClassCastException for any elements e1 and e2 in the range). This sort is guaranteed to be _stable_ : equal elements will not be reordered as a result of the sort.  Implementation note: This implementation is a stable, adaptive, iterative mergesort that requires far fewer than n lg(n) comparisons when the input array is partially sorted, while offering the performance of a traditional mergesort when the input array is randomly ordered. If the input array is nearly sorted, the implementation requires approximately n comparisons. Temporary storage requirements vary from a small constant for nearly sorted input arrays to n/2 object references for randomly ordered input arrays.  The implementation takes equal advantage of ascending and descending order in its input array, and can take advantage of ascending and descending order in different parts of the the same input array. It is well-suited to merging two or more sorted arrays: simply concatenate the arrays and sort the resulting array.  The implementation was adapted from Tim Peters's list sort for Python ([ TimSort](http://svn.python.org/projects/python/trunk/Objects/listsort.txt)). It uses techniques from Peter McIlroy's "Optimistic Sorting and Information Theoretic Complexity", in Proceedings of the Fourth Annual ACM-SIAM Symposium on Discrete Algorithms, pp 467-474, January 1993. **Type Parameters:** Type Parameter Name | Type Parameter Description  
---|---  
T  |  the class of the objects to be sorted  
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  the array to be sorted  
fromIndex  |  the index of the first element (inclusive) to be sorted  
toIndex  |  the index of the last element (exclusive) to be sorted  
c  |  the comparator to determine the order of the array. A null value indicates that the elements' [Comparable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/comparable-0000001054558443) should be used.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[ClassCastException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/classcastexception-0000001054678419) |  if the array contains elements that are not _mutually comparable_ using the specified comparator.  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  if fromIndex > toIndex or (optional) if the comparator is found to violate the [Comparator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/comparator-0000001054558585) contract  
[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrayindexoutofboundsexception-0000001054558451) |  if fromIndex < 0 or toIndex > a.length  
[h2]parallelPrefix public static <T> void parallelPrefix​(T[] array, [BinaryOperator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/binaryoperator-0000001054119953)<T> op) Cumulates, in parallel, each element of the given array in place, using the supplied function. For example if the array initially holds [2, 1, 0, 3] and the operation performs addition, then upon return the array holds [2, 3, 3, 6]. Parallel prefix computation is usually more efficient than sequential loops for large arrays. **Type Parameters:** Type Parameter Name | Type Parameter Description  
---|---  
T  |  the class of the objects in the array  
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
array  |  the array, which is modified in-place by this method  
op  |  a side-effect-free, associative function to perform the cumulation  
**Throws:** [Spliterator#SUBSIZED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__SUBSIZED), [Spliterator#ORDERED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__ORDERED), and [Spliterator#IMMUTABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__IMMUTABLE). **Type Parameters:** | Type Parameter Name | Type Parameter Description  
---|---  
T  |  type of elements  
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
array  |  the array, assumed to be unmodified during use  
startInclusive  |  the first index to cover, inclusive  
endExclusive  |  index immediately past the last index to cover  
**Returns:** a spliterator for the array elements **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrayindexoutofboundsexception-0000001054558451) |  if startInclusive is negative, endExclusive is less than startInclusive, or endExclusive is greater than the array size  
**Since:** 1.8 [h2]spliterator public static [Spliterator.OfInt](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator_ofint-0000001054558589) spliterator​(int[] array) Returns a [Spliterator.OfInt](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator_ofint-0000001054558589) covering all of the specified array. The spliterator reports [Spliterator#SIZED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__SIZED), [Spliterator#SUBSIZED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__SUBSIZED), [Spliterator#ORDERED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__ORDERED), and [Spliterator#IMMUTABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__IMMUTABLE). **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
array  |  the array, assumed to be unmodified during use  
**Returns:** a spliterator for the array elements **Since:** 1.8 [h2]spliterator public static [Spliterator.OfInt](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator_ofint-0000001054558589) spliterator​(int[] array, int startInclusive, int endExclusive) Returns a [Spliterator.OfInt](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator_ofint-0000001054558589) covering the specified range of the specified array. The spliterator reports [Spliterator#SIZED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__SIZED), [Spliterator#SUBSIZED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__SUBSIZED), [Spliterator#ORDERED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__ORDERED), and [Spliterator#IMMUTABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/spliterator-0000001054678588#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678588__IMMUTABLE). **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
array  |  the array, assumed to be unmodified during use | fromIndex  |  the index of the first element (inclusive) to be searched  
toIndex  |  the index of the last element (exclusive) to be searched  
key  |  the value to be searched for  
**Returns:** index of the search key, if it is contained in the array within the specified range; otherwise, (-(_insertion point_) - 1). The _insertion point_ is defined as the point at which the key would be inserted into the array: the index of the first element in the range greater than the key, or toIndex if all elements in the range are less than the specified key. Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  if fromIndex > toIndex  
[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrayindexoutofboundsexception-0000001054558451) |  if fromIndex < 0 or toIndex > a.length

Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[ClassCastException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/classcastexception-0000001054678419) |  if the range contains elements that are not _mutually comparable_ using the specified comparator, or the search key is not comparable to the elements in the range using this comparator.  
op) Performs [parallelPrefix(long[],java.util.function.LongBinaryOperator)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrays-0000001054678559#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678559__parallelPrefix-long:A-java_util_function_LongBinaryOperator-) for the given subrange of the array. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
array  |  the array  
fromIndex  |  the index of the first element, inclusive  
toIndex  |  the index of the last element, exclusive  
op  |  a side-effect-free, associative function to perform the cumulation  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  if fromIndex > toIndex  
[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrayindexoutofboundsexception-0000001054558451) |  if fromIndex < 0 or toIndex > array.length  
[NullPointerException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/nullpointerexception-0000001054119710) |  if the specified array or function is null  
**Since:** 1.8 [h2]parallelPrefix public static void parallelPrefix​(double[] array, [DoubleBinaryOperator](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/doublebinaryoperator-0000001054518638) op) Cumulates, in parallel, each element of the given array in place, using the supplied function. For example if the array initially holds [2.0, 1.0, 0.0, 3.0] and the operation performs addition, then upon return the array holds [2.0, 3.0, 3.0, 6.0]. Parallel prefix computation is usually more efficient than sequential loops for large arrays.  Because floating-point operations may not be strictly associative, the returned result may not be identical to the value that would be obtained if the operation was performed sequentially. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
array  |  the array, which is modified in-place by this method  
op  |  a side-effect-free function to perform the cumulation  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[NullPointerException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/nullpointerexception-0000001054119710) |  the array to be searched  
fromIndex  |  the index of the first element (inclusive) to be searched  
toIndex  |  the index of the last element (exclusive) to be searched  
key  |  the value to be searched for  
**Returns:** index of the search key, if it is contained in the array within the specified range; otherwise, (-(_insertion point_) - 1). The _insertion point_ is defined as the point at which the key would be inserted into the array: the index of the first element in the range greater than the key, or toIndex if all elements in the range are less than the specified key. Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  if fromIndex > toIndex  
[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrayindexoutofboundsexception-0000001054558451) |  if fromIndex < 0 or toIndex > a.length  
**Since:** 1.6 [h2]binarySearch public static int binarySearch​(double[] a, double key) Searches the specified array of doubles for the specified value using the binary search algorithm. The array must be sorted (as by the [sort(double[])](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrays-0000001054678559#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678559__sort-double:A-) method) prior to making this call. If it is not sorted, the results are undefined. If the array contains multiple elements with the specified value, there is no guarantee which one will be found. This method considers all NaN values to be equivalent and equal. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  the array to be searched  
key  |  the value to be searched for  
**Returns:** index of the search key, if it is contained in the array; otherwise, (-(_insertion point_) - 1). The _insertion point_ is defined as the point at which the key would be inserted into the array: the index of the first element greater than the key, or a.length if all elements in the array are less than the specified key. Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. **Since:** 1.6 [h2]equals public static boolean equals​(long[] a, long[] a2) Returns true if the two specified arrays of longs are _equal_ to one another. Two arrays are considered equal if both arrays contain the same number of elements, and all corresponding pairs of elements in the two arrays are equal. In other words, two arrays are equal if they contain the same elements in the same order. Also, two array references are considered equal if both are null. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  one array to be tested for equality  
a2  |  the other array to be tested for equality

**Returns:** true if the two arrays are equal [h2]equals public static boolean equals​(double[] a, double[] a2) Returns true if the two specified arrays of doubles are _equal_ to one another. Two arrays are considered equal if both arrays contain the same number of elements, and all corresponding pairs of elements in the two arrays are equal. In other words, two arrays are equal if they contain the same elements in the same order. Also, two array references are considered equal if both are null. Two doubles d1 and d2 are considered equal if: 
    
    new Double(d1).equals(new Double(d2))

(Unlike the == operator, this method considers NaN equals to itself, and 0.0d unequal to -0.0d.) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  one array to be tested for equality  
a2  |  the other array to be tested for equality  
startInclusive  |  the first index to cover, inclusive  
endExclusive  |  index immediately past the last index to cover  
**Returns:** a spliterator for the array elements **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrayindexoutofboundsexception-0000001054558451) |  if startInclusive is negative, endExclusive is less than startInclusive, or endExclusive is greater than the array size

[h2]binarySearch public static int binarySearch​(long[] a, int fromIndex, int toIndex, long key) Searches a range of the specified array of longs for the specified value using the binary search algorithm. The range must be sorted (as by the [sort(long[],int,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrays-0000001054678559#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678559__sort-long:A-int-int-) method) prior to making this call. If it is not sorted, the results are undefined. If the range contains multiple elements with the specified value, there is no guarantee which one will be found. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  the array to be searched  
fromIndex  |  the index of the first element (inclusive) to be searched

**Returns:** true if the two arrays are equal **See Also:** [Double.equals(Object)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/double-0000001054678413#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678413__equals-java_lang_Object-) [h2]equals public static boolean equals​(float[] a, float[] a2) Returns true if the two specified arrays of floats are _equal_ to one another. Two arrays are considered equal if both arrays contain the same number of elements, and all corresponding pairs of elements in the two arrays are equal. In other words, two arrays are equal if they contain the same elements in the same order. Also, two array references are considered equal if both are null. Two floats f1 and f2 are considered equal if: 
    
    new Float(f1).equals(new Float(f2))

(Unlike the == operator, this method considers NaN equals to itself, and 0.0f unequal to -0.0f.) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  one array to be tested for equality  
a2  |  the other array to be tested for equality  
**Returns:** true if the two arrays are equal **See Also:** [Float.equals(Object)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/float-0000001054358432#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358432__equals-java_lang_Object-) [h2]equals public static boolean equals​([Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446)[] a, [Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446)[] a2) Returns true if the two specified arrays of Objects are _equal_ to one another. The two arrays are considered equal if both arrays contain the same number of elements, and all corresponding pairs of elements in the two arrays are equal. Two objects e1 and e2 are considered _equal_ if (e1==null ? e2==null : e1.equals(e2)). In other words, the two arrays are equal if they contain the same elements in the same order. Also, two array references are considered equal if both are null. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  one array to be tested for equality  
a2  |  the other array to be tested for equality

[h2]fill public static void fill​(long[] a, int fromIndex, int toIndex, long val) Assigns the specified long value to each element of the specified range of the specified array of longs. The range to be filled extends from index fromIndex, inclusive, to index toIndex, exclusive. (If fromIndex==toIndex, the range to be filled is empty.) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  the array to be filled  
fromIndex  |  the index of the first element (inclusive) to be filled with the specified value  
toIndex  |  the index of the last element (exclusive) to be filled with the specified value  
val  |  the value to be stored in all elements of the array

**Returns:** index of the search key, if it is contained in the array within the specified range; otherwise, (-(_insertion point_) - 1). The _insertion point_ is defined as the point at which the key would be inserted into the array: the index of the first element in the range greater than the key, or toIndex if all elements in the range are less than the specified key. Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  if fromIndex > toIndex  
[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrayindexoutofboundsexception-0000001054558451) |  if fromIndex < 0 or toIndex > a.length  
**Since:** 1.6 [h2]binarySearch public static int binarySearch​(int[] a, int key) Searches the specified array of ints for the specified value using the binary search algorithm. The array must be sorted (as by the [sort(int[])](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrays-0000001054678559#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678559__sort-int:A-) method) prior to making this call. If it is not sorted, the results are undefined. If the array contains multiple elements with the specified value, there is no guarantee which one will be found. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  the array to be searched  
key  |  the value to be searched for  
**Returns:** index of the search key, if it is contained in the array; otherwise, (-(_insertion point_) - 1). The _insertion point_ is defined as the point at which the key would be inserted into the array: the index of the first element greater than the key, or a.length if all elements in the array are less than the specified key. Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. [h2]binarySearch public static int binarySearch​(int[] a, int fromIndex, int toIndex, int key) Searches a range of the specified array of ints for the specified value using the binary search algorithm. [h2]fill public static void fill​(int[] a, int fromIndex, int toIndex, int val) Assigns the specified int value to each element of the specified range of the specified array of ints. [h2]fill public static void fill​(byte[] a, byte val) the array to be searched  
key  |  the value to be searched for  
**Returns:** index of the search key, if it is contained in the array; otherwise, (-(_insertion point_) - 1). The _insertion point_ is defined as the point at which the key would be inserted into the array: the index of the first element greater than the key, or a.length if all elements in the array are less than the specified key. Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. [h2]binarySearch public static int binarySearch​(float[] a, int fromIndex, int toIndex, float key) Searches a range of the specified array of floats for the specified value using the binary search algorithm. The range must be sorted (as by the [sort(float[],int,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrays-0000001054678559#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678559__sort-float:A-int-int-) method) prior to making this call. If it is not sorted, the results are undefined. If the range contains multiple elements with the specified value, there is no guarantee which one will be found. This method considers all NaN values to be equivalent and equal. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  the array to be searched  
fromIndex  |  the index of the first element (inclusive) to be searched  
toIndex  |  the index of the last element (exclusive) to be searched  
key  |  the value to be searched for  
**Returns:** index of the search key, if it is contained in the array within the specified range; otherwise, (-(_insertion point_) - 1). The _insertion point_ is defined as the point at which the key would be inserted into the array: the index of the first element in the range greater than the key, or toIndex if all elements in the range are less than the specified key. Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  if fromIndex > toIndex  
[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrayindexoutofboundsexception-0000001054558451) |  if fromIndex < 0 or toIndex > a.length  
**Since:** 1.6 [h2]binarySearch public static int binarySearch​([Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446)[] a, [Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) key) Searches the specified array for the specified object using the binary search algorithm. The array must be sorted into ascending order according to the [Comparable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/comparable-0000001054558443) of its elements (as by the [sort(java.lang.Object[])](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/arrays-0000001054678559#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678559__sort-java_lang_Object:A-) method) prior to making this call. If it is not sorted, the results are undefined. (If the array contains elements that are not mutually comparable (for example, strings and integers), it _cannot_ be sorted according to the natural ordering of its elements, hence results are undefined.) If the array contains multiple elements equal to the specified object, there is no guarantee which one will be found. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
a  |  the array to be searched  
key  |  the value to be searched for  
**Returns:** index of the search key, if it is contained in the array; otherwise, (-(_insertion point_) - 1). The _insertion point_ is defined as the point at which the key would be inserted into the array: the index of the first element greater than the key, or a.length if all elements in the array are less than the specified key. Note that this guarantees that the return value will be >= 0 if and only if the key is found. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[ClassCastException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/classcastexception-0000001054678419) |  if the search key is not comparable to the elements of the array.  
[h2]binarySearch public static int binarySearch​([Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446)[] a, int fromIndex, int toIndex, [Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) key) Searches a range of the specified array for the specified object using the binary search algorithm. If it is not sorted, the results are undefined. (If the range contains elements that are not mutually comparable (for example, strings and integers), it _cannot_ be sorted according to the natural ordering of its elements, hence results are undefined.)
